S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(w^{L(0)-h_{1}}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle
vanishes for all
u_{3}\in N^{3}(0)
,
v\in M^{1}
, and
u_{2}\in M^{2}(0)
.
Indeed, for homogeneous
v\in M^{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg v}\right)=\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}w^{\deg{v}+h}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\right)w^{\deg{v}+h}+S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(w^{\deg{v}+h}\right),

by the
L_{(-1)}
-property

2.25
,

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(L_{(-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}}+(\deg{v}+h)S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}-1},

by the
associativity

2.26
,

\displaystyle=\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(\upomega,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}+(\deg{v}+h)S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}-1}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(\upomega,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle zw^{\deg{v}-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\qquad-\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(\upomega,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle(z-w)w^{\deg{v}-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}+\cdots,

applying the
Lemma 2.18

(Residue Sum Formula)
.
to
S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(\upomega,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle zw^{\deg{v}-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}
, and noticing that its possible poles on
\mathbb{P}^{1}
are
0
,
\infty
, and
\mathfrak{q}
,

\displaystyle=-(\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}+\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\infty})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(\upomega,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle zw^{\deg{v}-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\qquad-\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(\upomega,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle(z-w)w^{\deg{v}-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}+\cdots,

by the
generating properties

2.28
and
2.29
, and the
associativity

2.26
again,

\displaystyle=-S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}L_{(0)}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}-1}+S\left\langle L_{(0)}u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}-1}

\displaystyle\qquad-S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(L_{(0)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}-1}+(\deg{v}+h)S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}-1}

\displaystyle=(-h_{2}+h_{3}-(\deg{v}+h_{1})+(\deg{v}+h))S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg{v}-1}=0.

It remains to prove the
recursive formulas

3.8
and
3.9
.

For any homogeneous
a\in V^{r}
, the function
S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}}
factors through
\mathbb{P}^{1}
and has only
n+3
possible poles:
0
,
\infty
,
{z}_{1},\cdots,{z}_{n}
, and
w
.
Expanding it at
\mathfrak{p}=0
and applying the
Lemma 2.18

(Residue Sum Formula)
.
, we obtain

0=\left(\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}+\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\infty}+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{p}_{k}}+\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}\right)S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}.

(3.10)

By
2.26
,
2.27
,
2.28
, and
2.29
, and
2.16
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}=TS\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}a_{(m+\frac{r}{T})}u_{2}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\infty}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}=-TS\left\langle\theta(a({\textstyle m+\frac{r}{T}}))u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{p}_{k}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots(a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle{=}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{i}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{p}_{k}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots(a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z_{k}^{m+\frac{r}{T}-i}(z-z_{k})^{i}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z_{k}^{m+\frac{r}{T}-i},

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle{=}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{i}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{m+\frac{r}{T}-i}(z-w)^{i}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{m+\frac{r}{T}-i},

where the involved summation is finite since
a_{(i)}a^{k}=0
and
a_{(i)}v=0
for
i\gg 0
.
Note that

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}a_{(m+\frac{r}{T})}u_{2}\right\rangle=0

\displaystyle\qquad\text{if}\qquad m+\tfrac{r}{T}>\operatorname{wt}{a}-1,

\displaystyle S\left\langle\theta(a({\textstyle m+\frac{r}{T}}))u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=0

\displaystyle\qquad\text{if}\qquad m+\tfrac{r}{T}<\operatorname{wt}{a}-1.

Then it follows from
3.10
and
Table

1
that

\displaystyle\iota_{\mathfrak{p}=0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{m\leqslant\lfloor\operatorname{wt}{a}-1-\frac{r}{T}\rfloor}z^{-1-m-\frac{r}{T}}\bigg(S\left\langle\theta(a({\textstyle m+\frac{r}{T}}))u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{i}z_{k}^{m+\frac{r}{T}-i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{i}w^{m+\frac{r}{T}-i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle\bigg)

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}o(a)\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\sum_{m+\frac{r}{T}\leqslant\operatorname{wt}{a}-1}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{i}z^{-m-\frac{r}{T}-1}z_{k}^{m+\frac{r}{T}-i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\sum_{m+\frac{r}{T}\leqslant\operatorname{wt}{a}-1}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{i}z^{-m-\frac{r}{T}-1}w^{m+\frac{r}{T}-i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}o(a)\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\iota_{\mathfrak{p}=0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{p}_{k})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\iota_{\mathfrak{p}=0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle\qquad\text{(by \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:ExpOfFpq})},

which implies
3.8
by the injectivity of
\iota_{\mathfrak{p}=0}
.
Finally, expanding
S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle
at
\mathfrak{p}=\infty
yields a proof of
3.9
.
∎

Remark 3.7
.

When
N^{3}
is the contragridient module of
M^{3}
, the
monomial property
follows from the
truncation property
of intertwining operator
I_{S}
. However, in general we cannot assume that
N^{3}
is a contragredient module of some
M^{3}
. See
2.8
.

By
Proposition

3.6
, there exists a linear map

\uppi:\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(N^{3},M^{1},M^{2})\right)\longrightarrow\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(N^{3}(0),M^{1},M^{2}(0)\right).

Proposition 3.8
.

If
M^{2}
and
N^{3}
are lowest-weight modules, then
\uppi
is injective.

Proof.

Let
S\in\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(N^{3},M^{1},M^{2})\right)
such that
\uppi(S)=0
. Then,
S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=0
for all
u_{3}\in N^{3}(0),v\in M^{1}
and
u_{2}\in M^{2}(0)
.
Let
M
be the subspace

M:=\left\{v_{2}\in M^{2}\nonscript\>\middle|\allowbreak\nonscript\>\mathopen{}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle=0\ \text{for all}\ u_{3}\in N^{3}(0),v\in M^{1}\right\}

Then
M^{2}(0)\subseteq M
.
For any
v_{2}\in M
, homogeneous
a\in V^{r}
, and
m\in\mathbb Z
, by
2.28
and
3.8
, we have

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}a_{(m+\frac{r}{T})}v_{2}\right\rangle=\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}\uppi(S)\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}\uppi(S)\left\langle u_{3}o(a)\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}-\operatorname{wt}a}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}\uppi(S)\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle z^{m+\frac{r}{T}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=0.

This implies that
M
is a submodule of
M^{2}
containing
M^{2}(0)
, hence
M=M^{2}
. Therefore

S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle=0,\qquad\text{for all}\qquad u_{3}\in N^{3}(0),v\in M^{1},v_{2}\in M^{2}.

Similarly, using
2.29
and
3.9
, we can show

S\left\langle v_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle=0,\qquad\text{for all}\qquad v_{3}\in N^{3},v\in M^{1},v_{2}\in M^{2}.

Suppose all the
(n+3)
-point functions in
S
vanish.
Then, for any
a\in V
, by
2.26
,

\displaystyle\iota_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle v_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in\mathbb Z}\left(\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle v_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z-w)^{k}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}\right)(z-w)^{-k-1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in\mathbb Z}S\left\langle v_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(k)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z-w)^{-k-1}=0,

where the last equality follows from the inductive hypothesis.
Since
\iota_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}
is injective,

S\left\langle v_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle=0,\qquad\text{for all}\qquad v_{3}\in N^{3},v\in M^{1},v_{2}\in M^{2}.

This shows the system of functions
S
vanishes.
∎

3.2
Extending restricted correlation functions from the bottom levels

In this subsection, our objective is to establish an isomorphism between the spaces of correlation functions associated to the datum
\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})
and
\Sigma_{1}(\overline{M}(U^{3}),M^{1},\overline{M}(U^{2}))
respectively. Here
U^{2}
(resp.
U^{3}
) is an irreducible left (resp. right)
A_{g}(V)
-module, and
\overline{M}(-)
assigns an
A_{g}(V)
-module (or
A_{g^{-1}}(V)
-module) to the associated
generalized Verma module
, as defined in
Dong et al. 1998
.

Recall that there is an epimorphism from
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)_{0}
to
A_{g}(V)
as Lie algebras. Hence any
A_{g}(V)
-module
U
can be regarded as an
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)_{0}
-module, where
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)
is the twisted Lie algebra
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)
in
2.4
. Let
U
be an
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)_{-}+\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)_{0}
-module by letting
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)_{-}
act trivially and consider the following induced module

{U}\big(\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)\big)\otimes_{{U}\big(\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)_{-}+\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)_{0}\big)}U.

Then the
generalized Verma module

\overline{M}(U)
generated by
U
is defined to be the quotient space of the above module modulo the submodule generated by all the coefficients of the
twisted Jacobi identity
.

Proposition

3.6
shows that any system of correlation functions associated to the datum
\Sigma_{1}(\overline{M}(U^{3}),M^{1},\overline{M}(U^{2}))
restricts to one for the datum
\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})
.
In the rest of
Section

3
, we prove that the converse is also true by adopting a similar method as in
Zhu 1996
;
Liu 2023
.

Extending
U^{2}

We denote the tensor product space
T(\mathcal{L}_{g}(V))\otimes U
by
M(U)
, where
T(\mathcal{L}_{g}(V))
is the tensor algebra of the twisted Lie algebra
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)
.
The space
M(U)
is spanned by

b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\otimes\cdots\otimes b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})\otimes u,\quad b^{i}\in V^{r_{i}},m_{i}\in\mathbb Z,u\in U,p\in\mathbb N.

(3.11)

Given a system of correlation functions
S\colon U^{3}\otimes\operatorname{Sym}\left(V,\cdots,V,M^{1}\right)\otimes U^{2}\to\mathcal{O}(\infty\Delta_{n})
, we first extend it to
U^{3}\otimes\operatorname{Sym}\left(V,\cdots,V,M^{1}\right)\otimes M(U^{2})
by

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\otimes\cdots\otimes b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=0}\cdots\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+p}=0}

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{+1})\cdots(b^{p},\mathfrak{p}_{+p})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z_{+1}^{\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}}\cdots z_{+p}^{\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+p}}\cdots\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}.

It is easy to show that such a family of functions
S
is well-defined using the
L_{(-1)}
-derivative property

2.24
.
Define the radical of the family
S
by

\displaystyle\operatorname{Rad}(S):=\left\{v_{2}\in M(U^{2})\nonscript\>\middle|\allowbreak\nonscript\>\mathopen{}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle=0\text{ for all }u_{3}\in U^{3}\right\}.

(3.12)

Then define
\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2}):=\bigcap_{S}\operatorname{Rad}(S)
, where
S
ranges over
\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})\right)
.
It is easy to see that for any
v_{2}\in M(U^{2})
, we have

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\otimes\cdots\otimes b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})\otimes v_{2}\right\rangle

(3.13)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=0}\cdots\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+p}=0}

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{+1})\cdots(b^{p},\mathfrak{p}_{+p})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle z_{+1}^{\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}}\cdots z_{+p}^{\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+p}}\cdots\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}.

Lemma 3.9
.

Let
S\in\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})\right)
, and let
\varphi
be as in
3.7
. If
\varphi=0
then
S=0
.

Proof.

Clearly,
S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=0
for any
u_{3}\in U^{3}
and
u_{2}\in U^{2}
. Suppose all the
(n+3)
-point functions
S
vanish. For any homogeneous
a\in V^{r}
, by
3.8
,

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{p}_{k})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

and the right-hand side is
0
by the induction hypothesis.
∎

Lemma 3.10
.

The following properties hold for
\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
:

(i).

b({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m})\otimes\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})\subseteq\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
for all
b\in V^{r}
and
m\in\mathbb Z
.

(ii).

U^{2}\cap\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})=0
, where
U^{2}
is viewed as
\mathbb C\otimes U^{2}\subseteq M(U^{2})
.

(iii).

b({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m})\otimes u_{2}\in\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
for all
b\in V^{r}
and
m\in\mathbb Z
such that
\deg b({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m})<0
.

(iv).

b({\textstyle\operatorname{wt}b-1})\otimes u_{2}-1\otimes[b]\cdot u_{2}\in\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
for
b\in V^{0}
.

Proof.

(i)
is clear.
For property
(ii)
, suppose there exists a nonzero
u_{2}\in U^{2}\cap\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
. Then for any system of correlation functions
S
with a linear functional
\varphi
as in
3.7
and any homogeneous
a\in V^{r}
,
u_{3}\in U^{3}
,
v\in M^{1}
, and
u_{2}\in U^{2}
, by
3.7
and
3.9
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes v\otimes([a]\cdot u_{2})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[a]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg v}

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{\operatorname{wt}a}w^{\deg v}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}-\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\frac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{\operatorname{wt}a}w^{\deg v}

\displaystyle=0.

On the other hand, by
(
Dong et al. 1998
, Lemma 2.1)
,
A_{g}(V)
is a quotient algebra of
A(V^{0})
. Hence
U^{2}=A(V^{0})u_{2}
, and
\varphi
vanishes on all the entire
U^{3}\otimes M^{1}\otimes U^{2}
. This implies
S=0
by
Lemma

3.9
, which is a contradiction.

For property
(iii)
, given any
u_{2}\in U^{2}
and any homogeneous
b\in V^{r}
with
\deg b({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m})<0
, by
3.8
and
3.13
, we have

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}b({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m})\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b,\mathfrak{p})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{\frac{r}{T}+m}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot b\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}z^{-\operatorname{wt}b+\frac{r}{T}+m}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b_{(i)}a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}F_{\operatorname{wt}{b}-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{p}_{k})z^{\frac{r}{T}+m}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}F_{\operatorname{wt}{b}-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})z^{\frac{r}{T}+m}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=0,

where the last equality follows from the fact that
F_{n,i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})z^{n}
is holomorphic at
\mathfrak{p}=0
.

For property
(iv)
, given any
u_{2}\in U^{2}
and any homogeneous
b\in V^{0}
, by
3.9
we have

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}b({\textstyle\operatorname{wt}b-1})\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b,\mathfrak{p})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{\operatorname{wt}b-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[b]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}z^{-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b_{(i)}a^{k},\mathfrak{p}_{k})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}F_{\operatorname{wt}{b}-1,i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{p}_{k})z^{\operatorname{wt}b-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}F_{\operatorname{wt}{b}-1,i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})z^{\operatorname{wt}b-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[b]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle.

In the following lemma, we use the same notation for the elements in
M(U^{2})
and their images in
M(U^{2})/\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
.

Lemma 3.11
.

For any
a\in V^{r}
,
b\in V^{s}
,
v_{2}\in M(U^{2})
, and
m,n,l\in\mathbb Z
, the following element of
M(U^{2})/\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
vanishes:

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{l}{i}(-1)^{i}a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m+l-i})\otimes b({\textstyle\frac{s}{T}+n+i})\otimes v_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{l}{i}(-1)^{l+i}b({\textstyle\frac{s}{T}+n+l-i})\otimes a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m+i})\otimes v_{2}

(3.14)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{j}(a_{(j+l)}b)({\textstyle\frac{r+s}{T}+m+n-j})\otimes v_{2}.

Note that the summations in
3.14
are finite by property
(iii)
of
Lemma

3.10
.

Proof.

Indeed, for any system of correlation functions
S
, we have

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{l}{i}(-1)^{i}a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m+l-i})\otimes b({\textstyle\frac{s}{T}+n+i})\otimes v_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=0}\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{l}{i}(-1)^{i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m+l-i}z_{+2}^{\frac{s}{T}+n+i}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=0}\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z_{+1}-z_{+2})^{l}z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m}z_{+2}^{\frac{s}{T}+n}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}

\displaystyle{=}\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}(\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=0}+\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=\mathfrak{p}_{+2}})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z_{+1}-z_{+2})^{l}z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m}z_{+2}^{\frac{s}{T}+n}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}

\displaystyle{=}\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z_{+1}-z_{+2})^{l}z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m}z_{+2}^{\frac{s}{T}+n}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=\mathfrak{p}_{+2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z_{+1}-z_{+2})^{l}z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m}z_{+2}^{\frac{s}{T}+n}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}

\displaystyle{=}\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{l}{i}(-1)^{l+i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle z_{+2}^{\frac{s}{T}+n+l-i}z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m+i}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=\mathfrak{p}_{+2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{j}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z_{+1}-z_{+2})^{l+j}z_{+2}^{\frac{r+s}{T}+m+n-j}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{l}{i}(-1)^{i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}b({\textstyle\frac{s}{T}+n+l-i})\otimes a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m+i})\otimes v_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a_{(l+j)}b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle z_{+2}^{\frac{r+s}{T}+m+n-j}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}

\displaystyle=S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{l}{i}(-1)^{i}b({\textstyle\frac{s}{T}+n+l-i})\otimes a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m+i})\otimes v_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{j}(a_{(j+l)}b)({\textstyle\frac{r+s}{T}+m+n-j})\otimes v_{2}\right\rangle.

Equality
\ast
follows from the residue sum formula since both of the following functions

\displaystyle\mathfrak{p}_{+1}

\displaystyle\mapsto\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z_{+1}-z_{+2})^{l}z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m}z_{+2}^{\frac{s}{T}+n}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+2}}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{p}_{+1}

\displaystyle\mapsto S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots(a,\mathfrak{p}_{+1})(b,\mathfrak{p}_{+2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle(z_{+1}-z_{+2})^{l}z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m}z_{+2}^{\frac{s}{T}+n}

factor through
\mathbb{P}^{1}
, and the second fucntion has one extra possible pole at
z_{+1}=z_{+2}
. Furthermore, at any common pole
\mathfrak{p}_{\ast}\neq 0
of these functions, we have

\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=\mathfrak{p}_{\ast}}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}\cdots=\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+2}=0}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=\mathfrak{p}_{\ast}}\cdots

since
\mathfrak{p}_{\ast}
is away from the divisor
\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=\mathfrak{p}_{+2}
. Equality
\ast\ast
follows from expanding
(z_{+1}-z_{+2})^{l}
at
\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=0
and
z_{+1}^{\frac{r}{T}+m}
at
\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=\mathfrak{p}_{+2}
, respectively.
∎

Remark 3.12
.

In particular, taking
l=0
in
Lemma

3.11
, we have

\displaystyle a({\textstyle m+\frac{r}{T}})\otimes b({\textstyle n+\frac{s}{T}})\otimes v_{2}-b({\textstyle n+\frac{s}{T}})\otimes a({\textstyle m+\frac{r}{T}})\otimes v_{2}

(3.15)

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{m+\frac{r}{T}}{j}(a_{(j)}b)({\textstyle\frac{r+s}{T}+m+n-j})\otimes v_{2}\pmod{\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})}.

Therefore,
M(U^{2})/\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
is a
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)
-module.
Furthermore,
(iv)
of
Lemma

3.10
allows us to unambiguously write
b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\cdots b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})u_{2}
for the image of
b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\otimes\cdots\otimes b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})\otimes u_{2}
in
M(U^{2})/\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
. Moreover, it is clear that
M(U^{2})/\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
is spanned by the following elements:

b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\cdots b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})u_{2},

(3.16)

where
u_{2}\in U^{2}
,
b^{i}\in V^{r_{i}}
,
m_{i}\in\mathbb Z
for all
i
, and
\deg b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\geqslant\cdots\geqslant\deg b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})
.

Definition 3.13
.

Denote
M(U^{2})/\operatorname{Rad}(U^{2})
by
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})
.
Define a vertex operator by

Y_{\widetilde{M}(U^{2})}:V\rightarrow\operatorname{End}(\widetilde{M}(U^{2}))[[z,z^{-1}]],\quad Y_{\widetilde{M}(U^{2})}(a,z)=\sum_{n\in\mathbb Z}a({\textstyle\frac{n}{T}})z^{-\frac{n}{T}-1},

(3.17)

where
a\in V
and
a(\frac{n}{T})\in\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)
for all
n\in\mathbb Z
.

Furthermore, we introduce a gradation on
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})
by

\deg\left(b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\cdots b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})u_{2}\right):=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\deg b^{i}({\textstyle\frac{r_{i}}{T}+m_{i}}),

(3.18)

where
b_{i}\in V^{r_{i}}
,
m_{i}\in\mathbb Z
, and
u_{2}\in U^{2}
.
Then
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})=\bigoplus_{m\in\mathbb N}\widetilde{M}(U^{2})(\frac{m}{T})
by the type of its spanning elements (
3.16
) and
(iii)
of
Lemma

3.10
.

Proposition 3.14
.

The pair
(\widetilde{M}(U^{2}),Y_{\widetilde{M}(U^{2})})
in
Definition

3.13
defines an admissible
g
-twisted module of conformal weight
h_{2}
.

Proof.

By
(i)
of
Lemma

3.10
, the vertex operator
Y_{\widetilde{M}(U^{2})}(-,z)
is well-defined.
Given
a\in V^{r}
, by the definition of gradation
3.18
, we have

a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+n})\widetilde{M}(U^{2})(\tfrac{m}{T})\subseteq\widetilde{M}(U^{2})(\tfrac{m}{T}+\deg a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+n})).

Hence
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})(\tfrac{m}{T})=0
for
m<0
by
(iii)
of
Lemma

3.10
.
This shows
a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+n})v_{2}=0
for
n\gg 0
. The Jacobi identity of
Y_{\widetilde{M}(U^{2})}
follows from
Lemma

3.11
.

By adopting a similar argument as
(
Liu 2023
, Proposition 3.6 and 3.7)
, together with the assumption that
[\upomega]
acts as
h_{2}\operatorname{id}
on
U^{2}
, we can show that
Y_{\widetilde{M}(U^{2})}
satisfies the vacuum property, and
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})=\bigoplus_{n\in\mathbb N}\widetilde{M}(U^{2}))(\frac{n}{T})
with the bottom level
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})(0)=U^{2}
.
Moreover, for any
a\in V^{0}
, its action on
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})(0)=U^{2}
agrees with the operator
o(a)=a_{(\operatorname{wt}a-1)}:=\operatorname{Res}_{z}z^{\operatorname{wt}a-1}Y_{\widetilde{M}(U^{2})}(a,z)
, and each
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})(\frac{n}{T})
is an eigenspace of
L_{(0)}=o(\upomega)
with the eigenvalue
\frac{n}{T}+h_{2}
.
∎

So far, we have extended
S
to
U^{3}\otimes\operatorname{Sym}\left(V,\cdots,V,M^{1}\right)\otimes\widetilde{M}(U^{2})
.
The last factor can be further extended to
\overline{M}(U^{2})
since
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})
is a quotient module of the generalized Verma module
\overline{M}(U^{2})
.

Now let
M^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})=U^{3}\otimes T(\mathcal{L}_{g}(V))
. By adopting a slight modification of the argument in this subsection, we can extend
S
to
M^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})\otimes\operatorname{Sym}\left(V,\cdots,V,M^{1}\right)\otimes\widetilde{M}(U^{2})
by

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\otimes b^{p}({\textstyle\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}})\otimes\cdots\otimes b^{1}({\textstyle\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=(-\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+1}=\infty})\cdots(-\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}_{+p}=\infty})

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(b^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{+1})\cdots(b^{p},\mathfrak{p}_{+p})\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z_{+1}^{\frac{r_{1}}{T}+m_{1}}\cdots z_{+p}^{\frac{r_{p}}{T}+m_{p}}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+p}}\cdots\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{+1}}.

Define
\operatorname{Rad}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})
as the intersection of all
\operatorname{Rad}^{\operatorname{op}}(S)
, where

\displaystyle\operatorname{Rad}^{\operatorname{op}}(S):=\left\{v^{\prime}_{3}\in M^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})\nonscript\>\middle|\allowbreak\nonscript\>\mathopen{}S\left\langle v^{\prime}_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}\cdots\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle=0\text{ for all }v_{2}\in\widetilde{M}(U^{2})\right\}.

(3.19)

Then
\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3}):=M^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})/\operatorname{Rad}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})
is a right
\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)
-module, and so a left
\mathcal{L}_{g^{-1}}(V)
-module via the pushout along
\theta\colon\mathcal{L}_{g}(V)\to\mathcal{L}_{g^{-1}}(V)
. Namely,
a({\textstyle\frac{m}{T}})v_{3}:=v_{3}\cdot\theta(a({\textstyle\frac{m}{T}}))
, where the anti-isomorphism
\theta
is defined in
2.7
.
Furthermore,
\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})
is an admissible
g^{-1}
-twisted module of conformal weight
h_{3}
whose vertex operator is given by

Y_{\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})}(a,z)v^{\prime}_{3}=\sum_{n\in\mathbb Z}a({\textstyle\frac{n}{T}})v^{\prime}_{3}z^{-\frac{n}{T}-1}=\sum_{n\in\mathbb Z}v^{\prime}_{3}\cdot\theta(a({\textstyle\frac{n}{T}}))z^{-\frac{n}{T}-1},

(3.20)

where
a\in V
and
v^{\prime}_{3}\in\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})
.
Then
S
is extended to
\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})\otimes\operatorname{Sym}\left(V,\cdots,V,M^{1}\right)\otimes\widetilde{M}(U^{2})
.
The first factor can be further extended to
generalized Verma module

\overline{M}(U^{3})
.

Proposition 3.15
.

The resulting system of correlation functions
S
satisfies the twisted genus-zero property associated to the datum
\Sigma_{1}(\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3}),M^{1},\widetilde{M}(U^{2}))
.

Proof.

It suffices to show the
truncation property
.
We first show that
S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}
is holomorphic at
\mathfrak{q}=0
for all
u_{3}\in U^{3}
, when
n\geqslant\deg v+\deg v_{2}
.
Since
\widetilde{M}(U^{2})
is a
V
-module by
Proposition

3.14
, we may assume
v_{2}=a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m})u_{2}
for some homogeneous
a\in V^{r}
,
m\in\mathbb Z
,
u_{2}\in U^{2}
, and
\deg v_{2}\geqslant 0
. Then

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m})u_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{\frac{r}{T}+m}w^{n}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{T}S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}z^{\frac{r}{T}+m-\operatorname{wt}a}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})z^{\frac{r}{T}+m}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle{=}\tfrac{1}{T}S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}z^{\frac{r}{T}+m-\operatorname{wt}a}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}-\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{\frac{r}{T}+m}{i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\frac{r}{T}+m+n-i}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{T}\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(u_{3}\cdot[a])\otimes v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle w^{n-\deg v}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=0}z^{\frac{r}{T}+m-\operatorname{wt}a}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}-\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{\frac{r}{T}+m}{i}\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes(a_{(i)}v)\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle w^{n-\deg v_{2}-\deg v},

which is holomorphic at
\mathfrak{p}=0
if
n\geqslant\deg v+\deg v_{2}
.

It remains to show
S\left\langle v^{\prime}_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}
is holomorphic at
\mathfrak{q}=0
for all
v^{\prime}_{3}\in\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3})
, when
n\geqslant\deg v+\deg v_{2}
.
We may assume
v^{\prime}_{3}=\theta(a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m}))u_{3}
for some homogeneous
a\in V^{r}
,
m\in\mathbb Z
,
u_{3}\in U^{3}
, and
-\operatorname{wt}a+\frac{r}{T}+m+1\geqslant 0
. Then

\displaystyle S\left\langle\theta(a({\textstyle\frac{r}{T}+m}))u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{1}{T}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\infty}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle z^{\frac{r}{T}+m}w^{n}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{1}{T}S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\infty}z^{\frac{r}{T}+m-\operatorname{wt}a}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}-\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\infty}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})z^{\frac{r}{T}+m}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle{=}-\tfrac{1}{T}S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle w^{n}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\infty}z^{\frac{r}{T}+m-\operatorname{wt}a}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{\frac{r}{T}+m}{i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}v_{2}\right\rangle w^{\frac{r}{T}+m+n-i}.

The first term is holomorphic at
\mathfrak{q}=0
since
u_{3}\cdot[a]\in U^{3}
and
n\geqslant\deg v+\deg v_{2}
. The second term is holomorphic at
\mathfrak{q}=0
since
\deg a_{(i)}v+\deg v_{2}=\operatorname{wt}a+\deg v-i-1+\deg v_{2}\leqslant\frac{r}{T}+m+n-i
.
∎

By
Proposition

3.15
, the extended system of
g
-twisted correlation functions
S\in\overline{M}(U^{3})

\otimes\operatorname{Sym}\left(V,\cdots,V,M^{1}\right)\otimes\overline{M}(U^{2})
also satisfies the
twisted genus-zero property
associated to the datum
\Sigma_{1}(\overline{M}(U^{3}),M^{1},\overline{M}(U^{2}))
.

Now we have our main theorem in this section:

Theorem 3.16
.

Let
M^{1}
be an admissible untwisted module of conformal weight
h_{1}
, and let
U^{2}
(resp.
U^{3}
) be a left (resp. right)
A_{g}(V)
-module with
[\upomega]
acting as
h_{2}
(resp.
h_{3}
). Put
h=h_{1}+h_{2}-h_{3}
.
Then any system of
g
-twisted restricted correlation functions associated to the datum
\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})
can be extended to one associated to the datum
\Sigma_{1}(\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3}),M^{1},\widetilde{M}(U^{2}))
and one associated to the datum
\Sigma_{1}(\overline{M}(U^{3}),M^{1},\overline{M}(U^{2}))
. Moreover, we have

\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})\right)\cong\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(\widetilde{M}^{\operatorname{op}}(U^{3}),M^{1},\widetilde{M}(U^{2}))\right)\cong\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(\overline{M}(U^{3}),M^{1},\overline{M}(U^{2}))\right).

Proof.

It follows from
Proposition

3.6
and
Proposition

3.15
.
∎

Corollary 3.17
.

Let
M^{2}
and
M^{3}
be admissible
g
-twisted
V
-modules of conformal weight
h_{2}
and
h_{3}
, and assume
M^{2}
and
(M^{3})^{\prime}
are generalized Verma modules.
Then

\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(M^{3}(0)^{\ast},M^{1},M^{2}(0))\right)\cong\operatorname{Cor}\textstyle\binom{M^{3}}{M^{1}\;M^{2}}\cong\mathfrak{I}\textstyle\binom{M^{3}}{M^{1}\;M^{2}}.

Proof.

It follows from
Theorem

3.16
and
Theorem

2.24
.
∎

4
Reconstructing
g
-twisted restricted correlation functions

4.1
g
-twisted restricted conformal blocks (first definition)

We introduce the following notion of space of restricted coinvariants and conformal blocks:

Definition 4.1
.

Let
M^{1}
be an admissible untwisted module of conformal weight
h_{1}
, and
U^{2}
(resp.
U^{3}
) a left (resp. right)
A_{g}(V)
-module on which
[\upomega]
acts as
h_{2}\operatorname{id}
(resp.
h_{3}\operatorname{id}
). Put
h=h_{1}+h_{2}-h_{3}
.
Let
J
be the subspace of
U^{3}\otimes M^{1}\otimes U^{2}
spanned by the elements

\displaystyle u_{3}\otimes(L_{(-1)}+L_{(0)}-h_{1}+h)v\otimes u_{2},

(4.1)

\displaystyle u_{3}\cdot[a]\otimes v\otimes u_{2}-\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a}{j}u_{3}\otimes a_{(j-1)}v\otimes u_{2},\quad a\in V^{0},

(4.2)

\displaystyle u_{3}\otimes v\otimes[a]\cdot u_{2}-\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1}{j}u_{3}\otimes a_{(j-1)}v\otimes u_{2},\quad a\in V^{0},

(4.3)

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\frac{r}{T}}{j}u_{3}\otimes a_{(j-1)}v\otimes u_{2},\quad a\in V^{r},r\neq 0,

(4.4)

where
u_{3}\in U^{3}
,
v\in M^{1}
, and
u_{2}\in U^{2}
.
We call the quotient space
(U^{3}\otimes M^{1}\otimes U^{2})/J
the
space of
g
-twisted restricted coinvariants
and a linear functional
\varphi
vanishing on
J
a
g
-twisted restricted conformal block
associated to
U^{3},M^{1},
and
U^{2}
. We denote the vector space of
g
-twisted restricted conformal blocks by
\operatorname{{C}}\left(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2}\right)
.

Remark 4.2
.

The relations
4.1
,
4.2
,
4.3
, and
4.4
are obtained from our later calculation of the twisted correlation functions. In fact, these relations are also compatible with the definitions of the usual space of (twisted) coinvariants and conformal blocks of VOAs associated to the datum
(\mathbb{P}^{1}_{\mathbb C},0,1,\infty,M^{2},M^{1},M^{3})
in
Zhu 1994
;
Frenkel and Ben-Zvi 2004
;
Frenkel and Szczesny 2004
;
Nagatomo and Tsuchiya 2005
. We can obtain relations
4.1
,
4.2
,
4.3
, and
4.4
by restricting
M^{2}
and
M^{3}
to their bottom levels. We will give a more general definition of
twisted (restricted) conformal block
and discuss it in more detail in a subsequent paper
Gao et al.
.

Remark 4.3
.

Observe that
\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a}{j}a_{(j-1)}v=a\ast v
and
\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1}{j}a_{(j-1)}v=v\ast a
for
a\in V^{0}
and
v\in M^{1}
, where
a\ast v
and
v\ast a
are the
A(V^{0})
-bimodule actions defined in
Frenkel and Zhu 1992
.
Later on, we will show that the vector space
\operatorname{{C}}\left(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2}\right)
is indeed dual to
U^{3}\otimes_{A_{g}(V)}B_{g,\lambda}(M^{1})\otimes_{A_{g}(V)}U^{2}
, where
B_{g,\lambda}(M^{1})
is a quotient of
A_{g}(M^{1})
constructed in
Jiang and Jiao 2016
that generalizes
B_{\lambda}(M^{1})
in
Liu 2023
and
\lambda=h_{2}-h_{3}
.

Proposition 4.4
.

Any systme of correlation functions
S\in\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})\right)
gives rise to a meromorphic family of linear functionas
\{\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})\}_{\mathfrak{q}\in\accentset{\circ}{C}}
in
\operatorname{{C}}\left(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2}\right)
.

Proof.

Given any
S\in\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})\right)
, we define a meromorphic family of linear functionas
\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})
on
U^{3}\otimes M^{1}\otimes U^{2}
by

\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})\colon u_{3}\otimes v\otimes u_{2}\in U^{3}\otimes M^{1}\otimes U^{2}\longmapsto S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(w^{L(0)-h_{1}}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle.

We show
\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})
vanishes on
J
. Vanishing of
\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})
on
4.1
follows from the
L_{(-1)}
-derivative property

2.25
.
For homogeneous
a\in V^{r}
in
J
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}{j}\left\langle\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes a_{(j-1)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}{j}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(j-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg v+\operatorname{wt}a-j}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}{j}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg v+\operatorname{wt}a-j}(z-w)^{j-1}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}{j}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{\deg v+\operatorname{wt}a-j}(z-w)^{j-1}z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}{j}\operatorname{Res}_{\mathfrak{p}=\mathfrak{q}}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T},i}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\phantom{=}\cdot w^{\deg v+\operatorname{wt}a-j}(z-w)^{j-1}z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}\mathinner{\mathrm{d}}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\left\langle\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\cdot[a]\otimes v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\sum_{l=0}^{i}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}{j}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}{i-l}\binom{-\operatorname{wt}a+1-\delta(r)-\frac{r}{T}}{l+1-j}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}\cdot\left\langle\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes a_{(i)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\left\langle\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\cdot[a]\otimes v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle.

The last equality follows from the identity
\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}{j}\binom{-\operatorname{wt}a+1-\delta(r)-\frac{r}{T}}{l+1-j}=0
for
l\geqslant 0
.
This shows that
\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})
vanishes on
4.2
(resp.
4.4
) when
r=0
(resp.
r\neq 0
). The vanishing of
\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})
on
4.3
can be proved by a similar method using the other recursive formula.
Hence
\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})
is in
\operatorname{{C}}\left(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2}\right)
for all
\mathfrak{q}\in\accentset{\circ}{C}
.

By the
monomial property
of
S
, the family
\{\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})\}_{\mathfrak{q}\in\accentset{\circ}{C}}
is constant. In particular, it is meromorphic.
∎

Theorem 4.5
.

\operatorname{{C}}\left(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2}\right)\cong\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})\right).

Proof.

Given any
S\in\operatorname{Cor}\left(\Sigma_{1}(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2})\right)
, by
Proposition

4.4
, we have a constant family
\{\varphi_{S}(\mathfrak{q})\}_{\mathfrak{q}\in\accentset{\circ}{C}}
of elements of
\operatorname{{C}}\left(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2}\right)
.

The rest of
Section

4
and the whole
Section

5
are dedicated to proving the opposite direction, i.e., given any
g
-twisted restricted conformal block
\varphi
, one can reconstruct a system of
g
-twisted correlation functions
S_{\varphi}
, such that
\varphi_{S_{\varphi}}=\varphi
and
S_{\varphi_{S}}=S
.
∎

4.2
Constructions of 3-point, 4-point, and 5-point functions

In this subsection, we construct the
g
-twisted
3
-point,
4
-point, and
5
-point functions based on a given restricted conformal block
\varphi\in\operatorname{{C}}\left(U^{3},M^{1},U^{2}\right)
. The general
(n+3)
-point functions can be inductively defined using the recursive formulas.

Construction 4.6
.

Define the
3
-point function

S_{M}\colon U^{3}\otimes M^{1}\otimes U^{2}\to\mathbb C[w^{\pm\frac{1}{T}}]
by the formula:

u_{3}\otimes v\otimes u_{2}\longmapsto S_{M}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle:=\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes w^{h_{1}-L(0)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle.

(4.5)

Next, we can define the
4
-point functions
S_{VM}^{L}:U^{3}\otimes V\otimes M^{1}\otimes U^{2}\rightarrow\mathcal{O}(\infty\Delta_{1})
and
S_{MV}^{R}:U^{3}\otimes M^{1}\otimes V\otimes U^{2}\rightarrow\mathcal{O}(\infty\Delta_{1})
as follows:

Construction 4.7
.

For homogeneous
a\in V^{r}
,
v\in M^{1}
,
u_{3}\in U^{3}
and
u_{2}\in U^{2}
, define the 4-point functions by

\displaystyle S_{VM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle:

\displaystyle=S_{M}\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})S_{M}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

(4.6)

\displaystyle S_{MV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a,\mathfrak{p})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle:

\displaystyle=S_{M}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[a]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})S_{M}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle.

(4.7)

Before we move on to construct
5
-point functions, we first prove the following lemma which states that the
4
-point functions we constructed satisfy the
locality
:

Lemma 4.8
.

S_{VM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{MV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a,\mathfrak{p})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle
.

Proof.

If
r\neq 0
, then
[a]=0
and
\delta(r)=0
. Hence
S_{VM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle
agrees with
S_{MV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle
.
Now suppose
a\in V^{0}
. Recalling
Lemma

3.4
,

F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1,i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})-F_{\operatorname{wt}a,i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})=\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1}{i}z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}w^{\operatorname{wt}a-i-1},

we thus have

\displaystyle S_{VM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle-S_{MV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a,\mathfrak{p})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle S_{M}\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}-S_{M}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[a]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\left(F_{\operatorname{wt}a-1,i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})-F_{\operatorname{wt}a,i}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{q})\right)S_{M}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\cdot[a]\otimes v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}w^{-\deg v}-\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes v\otimes[a]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}w^{-\deg v}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1}{i}z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}w^{\deg v}\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes a_{(i)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle 0.

The last equality follows from
4.2
and
4.3
.
∎

Construction 4.9
.

For
5
-point functions, we define
S_{VVM}^{L}
,
S_{VMV}^{L}
by

\displaystyle S_{VVM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{VMV}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a^{1}]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1+\delta(r)+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{p}_{2})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1}_{(i)}a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

(4.8)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1+\delta(r)+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(a^{1}_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

and
S_{MVV}^{R}
,
S_{VMV}^{R}
by

\displaystyle S_{VMV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{MVV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[a^{1}]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle z^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{p}_{2})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1}_{(i)}a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

(4.9)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(a^{1}_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

where
a^{1}\in V^{r}
,
a^{2}\in V^{s}
are homogeneous,
v\in M^{1}
,
u_{3}\in U^{3}
,
u_{2}\in U^{2}
, and
S
is the
4
-point function defined in

.

For the well-definedness of the 5-point functions, we need to show that

S_{VMV}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{VMV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

(4.10)

and for the proof of
locality
of the 5-point functions, we need to show that

\displaystyle S_{VMV}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{VMV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

(4.11)

\displaystyle S_{VVM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{VVM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

(4.12)

\displaystyle S_{MVV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{MVV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle.

(4.13)

Proposition 4.10
.

Assume
4.11
and
4.12
hold, then
4.10
holds. Assume
4.11
and
4.10
hold, then
4.13
holds.

Proof.

The proof of the first part is similar to
(
Liu 2023
, Proposition 4.12)
.
Now assume
4.11
and
4.10
hold, then

\displaystyle S_{MVV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{VMV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=S_{VMV}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=S_{VMV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=S_{MVV}^{R}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

where the first and last equality follow from
4.9
, the second equality follows from
4.11
, and the third equality follows from
4.10
. Thus
4.13
holds.
∎

By

, to show

and

above satisfies

Proposition 4.12
.

For
a^{1},a^{2}\in V
, we have:

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(L_{(-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\right)

(4.14)

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(L_{(-1)}a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{1}}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

(4.15)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\right),

(4.16)

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(L_{(-1)}a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{1}}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle,

(4.17)

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(L_{(-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\right).

(4.18)

Proof.

We first show
4.17
. By
3.5
, It is straightforward to show that

\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}F_{n,i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})=(i+1)F_{n,i+1}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q}),\quad\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{1}}}F_{n,i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})=-(i+1)F_{n+1,i+1}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q}).

(4.19)

Note that
[L_{(-1)}a+L_{(0)}a]=0
, and
(L_{(-1)}a)_{(i)}=-ia_{(i-1)}
for
a\in V
or
a\in M^{1}
. Suppose
a^{1}\in V^{r}
, we have

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(L_{(-1)}a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[L_{(-1)}a^{1}]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}((L_{(-1)}a^{1})_{(i)}a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})((L_{(-1)}a^{1})_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[a^{1}]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\geqslant 0}iF_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1}_{(i-1)}a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\geqslant 0}iF_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(a^{1}_{(i-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{z_{1}}}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle.

Thus
4.17
holds. It’s easy to see that the
4
-point and
5
-point functions we defined satisfy the
vacuum property
in
Definition

3.5
. Letting

in

, we get
4.15
.

For
4.14
, note that
\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes L_{(-1)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle=-\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes(\deg v+h)v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle,
hence

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(L_{(-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}=\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes L_{(-1)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-\deg v-1-h}

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes(\deg v+h)v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-\deg v-1-h}=\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\right).

For
4.16
, note that
a^{1}_{(i)}L_{(-1)}v=L_{(-1)}a^{1}_{(i)}v+ia^{1}_{(i-1)}v.
Therefore,

\displaystyle S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(L_{(-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}=S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(L_{(-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[a^{1}]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1}_{(i)}L_{(-1)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1}_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1+\tfrac{r}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})\right)S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1}_{(i)}v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}}{\mathinner{\partial}\mathchoice{\mskip-3.0mu}{\mskip-3.0mu}{}{}{}{w}}\left(S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle w^{-h}\right).

4.18
can be proved in a similar way.
∎

We conclude this subsection by giving a proof of
Lemma

4.11
.

Proof of
Lemma

4.11
.

We first show
4.11
. Suppose
a^{1}\in V^{r}
and
a^{2}\in V^{s}
. If
r=s=0
, the proof follows the same suit as the argument in Section 4.2 in
Liu 2023
. Note that the only property of
\varphi
we need to use to prove
4.11
is the equality

\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\cdot[a]\otimes v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle-\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes v\otimes[a]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle=\sum_{j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a-1}{j}\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes a_{(j-1)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle,

which, in our case, follows from
4.2

and
4.3
.

If
r\neq 0
, then
4.11
holds by
4.8
and
4.9
. So it suffices to deal with the case where
r=0
and
s\neq 0
.
Similar to Lemma 4.13 in
Liu 2023
, we have the following formula on module
M^{1}
:

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}{j}\binom{\tfrac{s}{T}+\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-1+n}{i}\left(a^{1}_{(j)}a^{2}_{(i)}-a^{2}_{(i)}a^{1}_{(j)}\right)v

(4.20)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}{j}\binom{\tfrac{s}{T}+\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-j-2+\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+n}{i}(a^{1}_{(j)}a^{2})_{(i)}v,

where
a^{1}\in V^{0},a^{2}\in V^{s}
, and
n\in\mathbb N
. For
u_{3}\in U^{3}
,
u_{2}\in U^{2}
, and
v\in M^{1}
, we write

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle:=S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a^{1}]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}-S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}[a^{1}]\cdot u_{2}\right\rangle z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}},

(4.21)

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j\geqslant 0}(F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1},j}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q})-F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1,j}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{q}))S\langle u_{3}\,|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1}_{(j)}v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\rangle,

(4.22)

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j\geqslant 0}(F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1},j}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{p}_{2})-F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1,j}(\mathfrak{p}_{1},\mathfrak{p}_{2}))S\langle u_{3}\,|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{1}_{(j)}a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\rangle.

(4.23)

By
Lemma

3.4
,
4.2
,
4.3
,
4.5
,
4.8
, and
4.9
, we can derive the following expressions for
A
,

, and

:

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}{j}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-1+\tfrac{s}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{2},\mathfrak{q})\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes a^{1}_{(j)}a^{2}_{(i)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}w^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+i+1-\deg v},

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i,j\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}{j}F_{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-1+\tfrac{s}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{2},\mathfrak{q})\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes a^{2}_{(i)}a^{1}_{(j)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}w^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+i+1-\deg v},

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle=-\sum_{j,i\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}{j}F_{\operatorname{wt}(a^{1}_{(j)}a^{2})-1+\tfrac{s}{T},i}(\mathfrak{p}_{2},\mathfrak{q})\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes(a^{1}_{(j)}a^{2})_{(i)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}z_{2}^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-j-1}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}\cdot w^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+j+1+i+1-\deg v}.

Then, by
4.20

\displaystyle\iota_{\mathfrak{p}_{2}=\infty}(A+B+C)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j\geqslant 0}\sum_{n\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}{j}\binom{\tfrac{s}{T}+\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-1+n}{i}\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes(a^{1}_{(j)}a^{2}_{(i)}v-a^{2}_{(i)}a^{1}_{(j)}v)\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\phantom{=}\cdot z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\tfrac{s}{T}-n}w^{\tfrac{s}{T}+n-\deg v}

\displaystyle-\sum_{i,j\geqslant 0}\sum_{n\geqslant 0}\binom{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-1}{j}\binom{\tfrac{s}{T}+\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-j-2+\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+n}{i}\left\langle\varphi\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{3}\otimes(a^{1}_{(j)}a^{2})_{(i)}v\otimes u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\phantom{=}\cdot z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\tfrac{s}{T}-n}w^{\tfrac{s}{T}+n-\deg v}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle 0.

Thus
A+B+C=0
, and
4.11
holds.

Now we show
4.12
. Again, the case
r=s=0
has been dealt with in
Liu 2023
. It suffices to show that
4.12
holds for the following three cases: (1)
r=0,s\neq 0
, (2)
r\neq 0,s\neq 0,
and
r+s\neq T
, or (3)
r\neq 0,s\neq 0,
and
r+s=T
.

Similar to (2.2.10) in
Zhu 1996
, we can rewrite the recursive formula
4.6
as follows:

\displaystyle S\langle u_{3}\,|\,\mathopen{}(a,\mathfrak{p})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\rangle=S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u\right\rangle_{2})z^{-\operatorname{wt}a}

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Res}_{x}\left(\frac{z^{-\operatorname{wt}a+1-\delta(r)-\frac{r}{T}}(w+x)^{\operatorname{wt}a-1+\delta(r)+\frac{r}{T}}}{z-w-x}S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a,x)v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle\right),

(4.24)

for
a\in V^{r}
homogeneous.

For the case where
r=0
and
s\neq 0
, by
Lemma

4.8
and
4.24
, we can express the left-hand side of
4.12
as follows:

\displaystyle S^{L}_{VVM}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle=\underbrace{S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[a^{1}]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}}_{(D1)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1}}\left(\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}}{z_{1}-w-x_{1}}\right)S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})v,\mathfrak{q})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2})\right\rangle}_{(D2)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\operatorname{Res}_{x_{0}}\left(\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}(z_{2}+x_{0})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}}{z_{1}-z_{2}-x_{0}}\right)S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})(Y(a^{1},x_{0})a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle}_{(D3)}

,

, we can rewrite

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1},x_{2}}\frac{z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+1-\frac{s}{T}+1}z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}}{(z_{2}-w-x_{2})(z_{1}(w+x_{2})-z_{2}(w+x_{1}))}

\displaystyle(D1)+(D2)+(D3)-(E1)-(E2)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1},x_{2}}\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+1-\frac{s}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{z_{1}(w+x_{2})-z_{2}(w+x_{1})}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{2}}\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1}}\left(\frac{z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+1-\frac{s}{T}}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{z_{2}-w-x_{2}}\cdot\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}}}{z_{1}-w-x_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle 0.

The proof of
4.12
for the case when

. By adopting a similar computation as above, using the fact that

\displaystyle S_{VVM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1}}\left(\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{s}{T}+1}(1+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}}{z_{1}-z_{2}-z_{2}x_{1}}\right)S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[Y(a^{1},x_{1})a^{2}]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle
