\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1},x_{2}}\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{s}{T}+1}(w+x_{2})^{\frac{s}{T}+\operatorname{wt}a^{2}}}{((w+x_{2})z_{1}-(w+x_{1})z_{2})(z_{2}-w-x_{2})}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\operatorname{Res}_{x_{2}}\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1}}\left(\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}}{z_{1}-w-x_{1}}\cdot\frac{z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+1-\frac{s}{T}}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{z_{2}-w-x_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

where

are the corresponding terms on the right-hand side. On the other hand, we can express the right-hand side of

\displaystyle S_{VVM}^{L}\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(a^{2},\mathfrak{p}_{2})(a^{1},\mathfrak{p}_{1})(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{2}}\left(\frac{z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+1-\frac{s}{T}}z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-\frac{r}{T}+1}(1+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{z_{2}-z_{1}-z_{1}x_{2}}\right)S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[Y(a^{2},x_{2})a^{1}]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Res}_{x_{2},x_{1}}\frac{z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+1-\frac{s}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}}z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-\frac{r}{T}+1}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{((w+x_{1})z_{2}-(w+x_{2})z_{1})(z_{1}-w-x_{1})}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\operatorname{Res}_{x_{2},x_{1}}\frac{z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+1-\frac{s}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}}z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-\frac{r}{T}+1}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{((w+x_{1})z_{2}-(w+x_{2})z_{1})(z_{1}-w-x_{1})}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1}}\operatorname{Res}_{x_{2}}\left(\frac{z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+1-\frac{s}{T}}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{z_{2}-w-x_{2}}\cdot\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}}{z_{1}-w-x_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

It is easy to see that

\displaystyle(F2)-(G3)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1},x_{2}}\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{s}{T}+1}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{((w+x_{2})z_{1}-(w+x_{1})z_{2})}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left(\frac{w+x_{2}}{z_{2}-w-x_{2}}-\frac{w+x_{1}}{z_{1}-w-x_{1}}\right)S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1},x_{2}}\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{s}{T}+1}(w+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}+\frac{s}{T}-1}}{(z_{2}-w-x_{2})(z_{1}-w-x_{1})}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(Y_{M^{1}}(a^{1},x_{1})Y_{M^{1}}(a^{2},x_{2})v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(G4).

\displaystyle(F3)-(G2)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1},x_{2}}\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}(w+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{s}{T}+1}(w+x_{2})^{\frac{s}{T}+\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-1}}{((w+x_{1})z_{2}-(w+x_{2})z_{1})(z_{2}-w-x_{2})}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-(F4).

. Finally, recall that
o(L_{(-1)}a+L_{(0)}a)=0
, which implies that

o(Y(a,x)b)=o((1+x)^{-\operatorname{wt}a-\operatorname{wt}b}Y\left(b,-x/(1+x)\right)a)
for

. Then, we have

\displaystyle(F1)=\operatorname{Res}_{x_{1}}\left(\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{s}{T}+1}(1+x_{1})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}}{z_{1}-z_{2}-z_{2}x_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[(1+x_{1})^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}}Y(a^{2},\frac{-x_{1}}{1+x_{1}})a^{1}]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{2}}\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{s}{T}+1}\left(\frac{1}{1+x_{2}}\right)^{\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+\frac{r}{T}-1}}{z_{1}-z_{2}+z_{2}\left(\frac{x_{2}}{1+x_{2}}\right)}\cdot\frac{-1}{(1+x_{2})^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1+x_{2}}\right)^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[Y(a^{2},x_{2})a^{1}]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{x_{2}}\left(\frac{z_{1}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{1}+1-\frac{r}{T}}z_{2}^{-\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{s}{T}+1}(1+x_{2})^{\operatorname{wt}a^{2}-\frac{r}{T}}}{z_{2}-z_{1}-z_{1}x_{2}}\right)S\left\langle u_{3}\cdot[Y(a^{2},x_{2})a^{1}]\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}(v,\mathfrak{q})\,\middle|\,\mathopen{}u_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(G1).

Therefore,
(F1)+(F2)+(F3)+(F4)-(G1)-(G2)-(G3)-(G4)=0
.

The proof of
